\phantomsection\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Course 15\quad Blockchain \& Decentralised AI}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=navy,colframe=navy,arc=2pt,left=8pt,top=5pt,bottom=5pt]{\small\bfseries\color{goldl}COURSE 15 \;|\; EAI-115 \;|\; EMERGING TECHNOLOGIES TRACK}\\[1pt]{\LARGE\bfseries\color{white}Blockchain \& Decentralised AI}\\[2pt]{\small\itshape\color{goldl}Verifiable data, model provenance and decentralised trust for AI ecosystems}\end{tcolorbox}
\par{\small\begin{tabularx}{\linewidth}{@{}>{\bfseries\cellcolor{rowbg}}p{2.3cm}Y>{\bfseries\cellcolor{rowbg}}p{2.3cm}p{2.6cm}@{}}Course Code & EAI-115 & L--T--P--C & 3--0--2--4\\Prerequisites & Data structures, cryptography basics, EAI-102 & Contact Hours & 45 L + 30 P\\\end{tabularx}}
\subsection*{Core Competencies}\begin{itemize}\small
\item Explain blockchain consensus, cryptography and smart-contract execution models.
\item Develop and test secure smart contracts and decentralised applications.
\item Use blockchain for AI data provenance, model registries, audit trails and data marketplaces.
\item Evaluate decentralised and privacy-preserving AI designs for enterprise and public digital infrastructure.
\end{itemize}
\subsection*{Five-Unit Curriculum}
\begin{tcolorbox}[unitbox,title={{\color{gold}UNIT I}\quad Blockchain Foundations\hfill{\color{gold}9 Hours}}]\small Distributed ledgers; hash functions, Merkle trees and digital signatures; consensus (PoW, PoS, PBFT, Raft); public, private and consortium blockchains; transactions, blocks and state; scalability trilemma; India Stack and digital public infrastructure context.\par\vspace{2pt}\noindent{\color{unitline}\leaders\hbox to 4pt{\hss\rule[0.6ex]{2pt}{0.4pt}\hss}\hfill\kern0pt}\par\textbf{Hands-on Outcome:} Builds a toy blockchain with hashing, Merkle trees and proof-of-work consensus.\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[unitbox,title={{\color{gold}UNIT II}\quad Smart Contracts and Decentralised Applications\hfill{\color{gold}9 Hours}}]\small Ethereum Virtual Machine; Solidity programming; token standards (ERC-20, ERC-721, ERC-1155); dApp architecture; oracles; Layer-2 rollups; smart-contract security (re-entrancy, overflow, access control) and auditing tools.\par\vspace{2pt}\noindent{\color{unitline}\leaders\hbox to 4pt{\hss\rule[0.6ex]{2pt}{0.4pt}\hss}\hfill\kern0pt}\par\textbf{Hands-on Outcome:} Writes and tests an ERC-20/721 contract in Hardhat and audits it with Slither.\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[unitbox,title={{\color{gold}UNIT III}\quad Enterprise and Permissioned Blockchains\hfill{\color{gold}9 Hours}}]\small Hyperledger Fabric architecture (channels, chaincode, endorsement); identity and membership services; supply-chain traceability; trade finance; credentials and verifiable identity (DIDs, verifiable credentials); interoperability between chains; performance and governance of consortium networks.\par\vspace{2pt}\noindent{\color{unitline}\leaders\hbox to 4pt{\hss\rule[0.6ex]{2pt}{0.4pt}\hss}\hfill\kern0pt}\par\textbf{Hands-on Outcome:} Deploys a Hyperledger Fabric supply-chain network and issues verifiable academic credentials.\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[unitbox,title={{\color{gold}UNIT IV}\quad Blockchain for Trustworthy AI\hfill{\color{gold}9 Hours}}]\small Data provenance and lineage on-chain; tamper-evident model registries and AI audit trails; content authenticity and deepfake tracing; decentralised data marketplaces and incentive design; federated learning with blockchain coordination; zero-knowledge proofs and verifiable inference (zkML) concepts.\par\vspace{2pt}\noindent{\color{unitline}\leaders\hbox to 4pt{\hss\rule[0.6ex]{2pt}{0.4pt}\hss}\hfill\kern0pt}\par\textbf{Hands-on Outcome:} Builds an on-chain AI model registry with IPFS artefacts and blockchain-coordinated federated learning.\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[unitbox,title={{\color{gold}UNIT V}\quad Decentralised AI Systems and Deployment\hfill{\color{gold}9 Hours}}]\small AI agents interacting with smart contracts; decentralised storage (IPFS) for datasets and models; decentralised compute networks; privacy (ZK, secure multi-party computation); regulatory considerations for crypto-assets and data in India; cost, energy and governance trade-offs; deploying enterprise blockchain + AI solutions.\par\vspace{2pt}\noindent{\color{unitline}\leaders\hbox to 4pt{\hss\rule[0.6ex]{2pt}{0.4pt}\hss}\hfill\kern0pt}\par\textbf{Hands-on Outcome:} Generates a zkML proof of a small model inference and deploys an agent that interacts with a smart contract.\end{tcolorbox}

\phantomsection\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Course 16\quad Forward Deployed Engineering (FDE)}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=navy,colframe=navy,arc=2pt,left=8pt,top=5pt,bottom=5pt]{\small\bfseries\color{goldl}COURSE 16 \;|\; EAI-116 \;|\; INDUSTRY PRACTICE TRACK}\\[1pt]{\LARGE\bfseries\color{white}Forward Deployed Engineering (FDE)}\\[2pt]{\small\itshape\color{goldl}Embedding with enterprise clients to discover, build, deploy and scale AI solutions that deliver measurable business value}\end{tcolorbox}
\par{\small\begin{tabularx}{\linewidth}{@{}>{\bfseries\cellcolor{rowbg}}p{2.3cm}Y>{\bfseries\cellcolor{rowbg}}p{2.3cm}p{2.6cm}@{}}Course Code & EAI-116 & L--T--P--C & 3--0--2--4\\Prerequisites & EAI-106 Large Language Models, EAI-107 Agentic AI, EAI-109 Data Engineering for AI; cloud and Git fundamentals & Contact Hours & 45 L + 30 P\\\end{tabularx}}
\subsection*{Core Competencies}\begin{itemize}\small
\item Translate a client's business problem into a scoped AI use case with clear KPIs, ROI and success criteria.
\item Prototype quickly on real customer data and prove value with an evaluation set built from the client's own examples.
\item Integrate and deploy AI securely inside customer environments: SSO, private networks, enterprise systems and compliance controls.
\item Run production systems, drive user adoption and feed field insights back to the product team as a trusted client partner.
\end{itemize}
\subsection*{Five-Unit Curriculum}
\begin{tcolorbox}[unitbox,title={{\color{gold}UNIT I}\quad The FDE Model and Customer Discovery\hfill{\color{gold}9 Hours}}]\small Origins of forward deployed engineering and how AI companies use it today; FDE vs software, solutions and ML engineers; stakeholder mapping; discovery interviews and process mapping; data and systems audit; framing use cases, KPIs, ROI and success criteria; scoping statements of work; build vs buy vs configure; consulting communication and trust-building.\par\vspace{2pt}\noindent{\color{unitline}\leaders\hbox to 4pt{\hss\rule[0.6ex]{2pt}{0.4pt}\hss}\hfill\kern0pt}\par\textbf{Hands-on Outcome:} Produces a discovery report for a client: process map, data audit, prioritised AI use cases and a scoped problem statement with measurable KPIs.\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[unitbox,title={{\color{gold}UNIT II}\quad Rapid Prototyping on Customer Data\hfill{\color{gold}9 Hours}}]\small Getting safe access to data in restricted environments; connectors for SQL, REST APIs, document stores and SaaS tools; building RAG and agent prototypes in days; demo-driven development; from notebook to usable app (Streamlit, Next.js); building evaluation sets from customer examples; handling messy, partial and sensitive data.\par\vspace{2pt}\noindent{\color{unitline}\leaders\hbox to 4pt{\hss\rule[0.6ex]{2pt}{0.4pt}\hss}\hfill\kern0pt}\par\textbf{Hands-on Outcome:} Delivers a working prototype on a client's sample data within one week, with an evaluation set and a live demo to stakeholders.\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[unitbox,title={{\color{gold}UNIT III}\quad Integration and Deployment in Customer Environments\hfill{\color{gold}9 Hours}}]\small Enterprise integration patterns; SSO with SAML/OIDC and role-based access; MCP servers and APIs to CRM, ERP and ticketing systems; deployment to customer VPC, on-premises and air-gapped environments; containers, Kubernetes and infrastructure as code; CI/CD; data residency, DPDP Act and security reviews.\par\vspace{2pt}\noindent{\color{unitline}\leaders\hbox to 4pt{\hss\rule[0.6ex]{2pt}{0.4pt}\hss}\hfill\kern0pt}\par\textbf{Hands-on Outcome:} Deploys the prototype into a simulated client VPC with SSO, infrastructure as code, CI/CD and a passed security checklist.\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[unitbox,title={{\color{gold}UNIT IV}\quad Production Reliability, Evaluation and Adoption\hfill{\color{gold}9 Hours}}]\small Observability, tracing and alerting; online evaluation and regression gates; guardrails and human-in-the-loop controls; incident response and SLAs; cost and latency management; user training and change management; adoption analytics; structured product feedback from the field to the core product team.\par\vspace{2pt}\noindent{\color{unitline}\leaders\hbox to 4pt{\hss\rule[0.6ex]{2pt}{0.4pt}\hss}\hfill\kern0pt}\par\textbf{Hands-on Outcome:} Publishes a runbook, a live dashboard of quality, adoption and cost, and a field-feedback memo with product recommendations.\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[unitbox,title={{\color{gold}UNIT V}\quad Enterprise Delivery, Value Realisation and Capstone\hfill{\color{gold}9 Hours}}]\small Measuring business impact against baseline; executive readouts and storytelling with data; expansion to new teams and use cases; handover, documentation and enablement; professional ethics, confidentiality and responsible AI in client work; career paths in FDE and solutions engineering.\par\vspace{2pt}\noindent{\color{unitline}\leaders\hbox to 4pt{\hss\rule[0.6ex]{2pt}{0.4pt}\hss}\hfill\kern0pt}\par\textbf{Hands-on Outcome:} Completes an end-to-end client engagement and presents an executive readout of measured business impact.\end{tcolorbox}

\item Rapid prototyping of RAG and agent solutions on real, messy customer data
\item Building evaluation sets from customer examples to prove a solution works
\item Enterprise integration: SSO (SAML/OIDC), APIs, MCP servers, CRM and ERP connectors
\item Deploying into customer VPC, on-premises and air-gapped environments with Kubernetes and IaC
\item Production operations: observability, guardrails, incident response and cost control
\item Driving user adoption, training and change management inside client teams
\item Executive communication: demos, ROI readouts and turning field insights into product feedback

Data Engineering for AI & Trustworthy AI, Artificial Intelligence & The fastest-growing AI role of 2026: embeds with clients to turn AI products into working, measured deployments.\\

\textbf{Quantum Software Engineer} & 11. Quantum Computing \& Quantum AI, 2. Machine Learning & Deep Learning & Builds hybrid quantum–classical solutions and quantum-safe systems.\\
\rowcolor{rowbg}\textbf{Blockchain + AI Engineer} & 15. Blockchain \& Decentralised AI, 10. Trustworthy AI & Data Engineering for AI & Builds verifiable data, model provenance and decentralised trust.\\

One flagship product per industry domain. Each combines several courses into a real-time, production-grade system that student teams can build as a capstone and that enterprises actually run. The KPIs are the measures each product improves, not promised results.

1 & Banking, Fintech \& Payments & \textbf{Real-Time Fraud \& Risk Intelligence Platform} & 2, 9, 3, 14, 10, 6, 16\\

5 & Logistics \& Supply Chain & \textbf{Autonomous Supply-Chain Control Tower} & 1, 2, 4, 7, 13, 15\\

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,colback=white,colframe=navy,boxrule=0.6pt,arc=2pt,title={\color{goldl}PRODUCT 1 \;|\; BANKING, FINTECH \& PAYMENTS\\\color{white}\large Real-Time Fraud \& Risk Intelligence Platform},colbacktitle=navy,fonttitle=\small\bfseries]
{\small\itshape\color{muted}Scores every payment in milliseconds and explains every block.}\par
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=l1bg,colframe=l1bg,arc=2pt,borderline west={3pt}{0pt}{saffron},top=2pt,bottom=2pt]\small\textbf{Business problem:} Digital payments such as UPI and cards move in seconds, and fraud moves with them. Rule engines miss new patterns and flood analysts with false alarms.\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=l3bg,colframe=l3bg,arc=2pt,borderline west={3pt}{0pt}{igreen},top=2pt,bottom=2pt]\small\textbf{What happens in real time:} Each transaction is scored as it streams in, within a sub-100 ms budget; risky ones are held, challenged or blocked before money leaves the account.\end{tcolorbox}
{\small\textbf{Product modules}}\begin{enumerate}\small
\item Streaming feature pipeline on transaction events (Kafka + feature store)
\item Gradient-boosted risk model with SHAP reason codes for every decision
\item Graph neural network that uncovers mule accounts and fraud rings
\item LLM analyst copilot that summarises cases and drafts suspicious-transaction reports
\item Model governance pack: drift monitoring, fairness tests and audit trail for regulators
\end{enumerate}
{\small\textbf{Courses that build it:} 2. Machine Learning, 9. Data Engineering for AI, 3. Deep Learning, 14. AI-Driven Cybersecurity, 10. Trustworthy AI, 6. Large Language Models, 16. Forward Deployed Engineering (FDE)\\\textbf{Technology stack:} Kafka, Spark Structured Streaming, Feast, XGBoost, PyTorch Geometric, FastAPI, MLflow, Neo4j\\\textbf{Business KPIs it moves:} Fraud caught before settlement, False-positive rate, p99 scoring latency, Analyst minutes per case\\\textbf{Job roles it showcases:} Machine Learning Engineer, Security Data Scientist, AI Data Engineer, Responsible AI Specialist, Forward Deployed Engineer\\\textbf{Context:} UPI, cards, net banking, RBI and DPDP Act compliance}
\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,colback=white,colframe=navy,boxrule=0.6pt,arc=2pt,title={\color{goldl}PRODUCT 2 \;|\; HEALTHCARE \& LIFE SCIENCES\\\color{white}\large Clinical Decision \& Medical Imaging Copilot},colbacktitle=navy,fonttitle=\small\bfseries]
{\small\itshape\color{muted}Triage scans, summarise records and flag risk, with the doctor in control.}\par
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=l1bg,colframe=l1bg,arc=2pt,borderline west={3pt}{0pt}{saffron},top=2pt,bottom=2pt]\small\textbf{Business problem:} Specialists are scarce outside big cities, scans wait in queues, and patient histories are scattered across reports.\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=l3bg,colframe=l3bg,arc=2pt,borderline west={3pt}{0pt}{igreen},top=2pt,bottom=2pt]\small\textbf{What happens in real time:} Scans are triaged as they are uploaded and urgent cases jump the queue; bedside vitals stream to early-warning models that alert care teams.\end{tcolorbox}
{\small\textbf{Product modules}}\begin{enumerate}\small
\item Deep-learning triage for retinal, chest X-ray and CT images with heat-map explanations
\item RAG assistant over patient records and clinical guidelines with cited answers
\item Early-warning score from streaming vitals at the edge
\item Federated learning so hospitals train together without sharing patient data
\item Clinician-in-the-loop review, consent and audit log
\end{enumerate}
{\small\textbf{Courses that build it:} 5. Computer Vision, 3. Deep Learning, 6. Large Language Models, 10. Trustworthy AI, 12. Edge AI, TinyML \& AIoT, 9. Data Engineering for AI, 16. Forward Deployed Engineering (FDE)\\\textbf{Technology stack:} PyTorch, MONAI, FHIR, LangGraph, pgvector, Flower (federated), NVIDIA Triton, ONNX\\\textbf{Business KPIs it moves:} Time to triage urgent scans, Sensitivity on referable cases, Clinician time saved per report, Explanation acceptance rate\\\textbf{Job roles it showcases:} Computer Vision Engineer, LLM Engineer, Responsible AI Specialist, Edge AI Engineer\\\textbf{Context:} ABDM health records, tele-ophthalmology, hospital PACS}
\end{tcolorbox}

\newpage\section*{Unicorn Track: 10 Frontier Products}\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Unicorn Track: 10 Frontier Products}
Ten frontier product ideas modelled on the categories that are producing AI unicorns in 2026. Each is deliberately complex: it combines 6 to 8 courses, several system layers and at least one hard technical moat. Built in stages, each starts as a 12-week team MVP and grows into a venture-scale platform, the kind of portfolio that wins top offers, research funding or start-up incubation.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay1,colframe=lay1,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 2. Agent orchestration:} planner, coder, tester, reviewer and deployer agents with shared memory\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay2,colframe=lay2,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 3. Code intelligence:} repository graph, embeddings over code and docs, retrieval of relevant files\end{tcolorbox}

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay4,colframe=lay4,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 5. Model layer:} routing between frontier APIs and fine-tuned open code models for cost and privacy\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay5,colframe=lay5,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 6. Trust layer:} secret scanning, licence checks, prompt-injection defence and full action audit\end{tcolorbox}
\subsection*{Hard Problems: The Moat}\begin{itemize}\small
\item Reliable long-horizon planning across large, real repositories
\item Evaluation harness that proves generated code works before a human sees it
\item Cost and latency control across many agent calls per task
\end{itemize}

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay0,colframe=lay0,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 1. Discovery:} inventory of models, datasets, prompts and agents across the company\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay1,colframe=lay1,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 2. Provenance:} signed model and dataset lineage (AI bill of materials) on a tamper-evident ledger\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay2,colframe=lay2,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 3. Red-teaming:} automated jailbreak, injection and poisoning tests before release\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay3,colframe=lay3,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 4. Runtime firewall:} inspects prompts, outputs and tool calls in real time\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay4,colframe=lay4,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 5. Policy engine:} who can use which model for what, with approvals\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay5,colframe=lay5,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 6. Compliance:} evidence packs for the EU AI Act, ISO/IEC 42001 and the DPDP Act\end{tcolorbox}
\subsection*{Hard Problems: The Moat}\begin{itemize}\small
\item Detection that keeps up with new attack techniques
\item Low-latency inline inspection
\item Trusted provenance that auditors accept
\end{itemize}
\subsection*{From Capstone to Company}
\begin{tcbraster}[raster columns=2,raster equal height,raster column skip=8pt]\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=l1bg,colframe=l1bg,arc=2pt,borderline north={2.5pt}{0pt}{saffron}]{\scriptsize\bfseries\color{muted}12-WEEK TEAM MVP}\\\small A proxy that sits in front of an LLM app, blocks prompt injection and data leaks, and logs every decision.\end{tcolorbox}\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=l3bg,colframe=l3bg,arc=2pt,borderline north={2.5pt}{0pt}{igreen}]{\scriptsize\bfseries\color{muted}VENTURE-SCALE PLATFORM}\\\small Enterprise AI security platform integrated with SOC tools and cloud AI services.\end{tcolorbox}\end{tcbraster}

AI-Driven Cybersecurity, 6. Large Language Models, 7. Agentic AI, 15. Blockchain \& Decentralised AI, 9. Data Engineering for AI, 2. Machine Learning\\\textbf{Technology stack:} LLM guard models, OPA (policy), Sigstore, Hyperledger Fabric, Garak / PyRIT, Envoy proxy, Elastic / SIEM, Go\\\textbf{Roles it prepares you for:} AI Security Engineer, AI Red-Team Engineer, AI Provenance Engineer, Detection Engineer}

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=navy,colframe=navy,arc=2pt,left=8pt,top=5pt,bottom=5pt]{\small\bfseries\color{goldl}UNICORN TRACK 9 \;|\; SPACE TECH \& GEOSPATIAL AI}\\[1pt]{\LARGE\bfseries\color{white}Space-to-Earth Geospatial Intelligence Platform}\\[2pt]{\small\itshape\color{goldl}Daily answers from satellite imagery for farms, insurers, cities and disaster response.}\\[2pt]{\small\color{white}Complexity {\color{goldl}★★★★☆} \;$\cdot$\; 7 courses \;$\cdot$\; 6 system layers}\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=l1bg,colframe=l1bg,arc=2pt,borderline west={3pt}{0pt}{saffron},top=2pt,bottom=2pt]\small\textbf{Why now:} Skyroot Aerospace became India's first space-tech unicorn in 2026, and satellite-imaging companies raised large rounds to expand constellations. {\color{muted}Category examples: Skyroot Aerospace, ICEYE.}\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=l3bg,colframe=l3bg,arc=2pt,borderline west={3pt}{0pt}{igreen},top=2pt,bottom=2pt]\small\textbf{What the product does:} A platform that ingests optical and radar satellite imagery, runs AI models to detect crops, floods, construction and damage, and delivers alerts and APIs to insurers, banks, governments and agribusiness.\end{tcolorbox}
\subsection*{System Architecture (top to bottom)}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay0,colframe=lay0,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 1. Ingestion:} multi-satellite optical and SAR feeds, tiling and cloud masking\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay1,colframe=lay1,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 2. Earth-observation models:} segmentation, change detection and object counting\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay2,colframe=lay2,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 3. Fusion:} combine satellite, weather, drone and ground-sensor data\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay3,colframe=lay3,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 4. Tasking optimiser:} choose which areas to image next (classical and quantum optimisation)\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay4,colframe=lay4,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 5. Products:} crop insurance claims, flood maps, loan collateral checks\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay5,colframe=lay5,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 6. Delivery:} APIs, alerts and map dashboards\end{tcolorbox}
\subsection*{Hard Problems: The Moat}\begin{itemize}\small
\item Models that work through clouds using radar
\item Ground-truth data partnerships
\item Speed from capture to customer answer
\end{itemize}

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=navy,colframe=navy,arc=2pt,left=8pt,top=5pt,bottom=5pt]{\small\bfseries\color{goldl}UNICORN TRACK 10 \;|\; AI FOR SCIENCE: DRUGS \& MATERIALS}\\[1pt]{\LARGE\bfseries\color{white}Autonomous Discovery Lab for Drugs \& Materials}\\[2pt]{\small\itshape\color{goldl}Generate, simulate and test new molecules in a closed loop.}\\[2pt]{\small\color{white}Complexity {\color{goldl}★★★★★} \;$\cdot$\; 7 courses \;$\cdot$\; 6 system layers}\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=l1bg,colframe=l1bg,arc=2pt,borderline west={3pt}{0pt}{saffron},top=2pt,bottom=2pt]\small\textbf{Why now:} Healthcare minted three new unicorns in February 2026, and AI-native healthcare and science companies remain among the most active categories in this year's unicorn class. {\color{muted}Category examples: AI-for-science labs (2026 cohort).}\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=l3bg,colframe=l3bg,arc=2pt,borderline west={3pt}{0pt}{igreen},top=2pt,bottom=2pt]\small\textbf{What the product does:} A closed-loop discovery system: generative models propose candidate molecules or materials, quantum and classical simulation screens them, robots run the most promising experiments, and results retrain the models.\end{tcolorbox}
\subsection*{System Architecture (top to bottom)}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay0,colframe=lay0,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 1. Generative design:} diffusion and graph models that propose molecules with target properties\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay1,colframe=lay1,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 2. Property prediction:} GNN surrogates for toxicity, binding and stability\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay2,colframe=lay2,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 3. Simulation:} quantum chemistry on simulators and real quantum hardware\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay3,colframe=lay3,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 4. Lab automation:} robotic liquid handling scheduled by an RL planner\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay4,colframe=lay4,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 5. Knowledge:} literature agent that mines papers and patents\end{tcolorbox}
\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=lay5,colframe=lay5,arc=2pt,top=1.5pt,bottom=1.5pt,left=6pt,before skip=2pt,after skip=2pt]\small\color{white}{\color{goldl}\bfseries 6. Data governance:} experiment provenance and IP records\end{tcolorbox}
\subsection*{Hard Problems: The Moat}\begin{itemize}\small
\item Proprietary experimental data from the closed loop
\item Speed from idea to validated candidate
\item IP generated by the platform
\end{itemize}
